> Heratio Help Center article. Category: Viewers & Media.

# Audio Player

## User Guide

Listen to audio recordings with enhanced playback features including waveform display, snippet creation, and transcription support.

---

## Creating Snippets

Save interesting sections for later reference:

### Step 1: Mark Start Point

While playing, click **Mark Start** when you hear the beginning of the section you want.

### Step 2: Mark End Point

Click **Mark End** when the section finishes.

### Step 3: Save Snippet
